
> pretest:visualtest
> VRT=1 npm run build:storybook


> build:storybook
> npm run -w storybook build


> prebuild
> npm run remove-dist


> remove-dist
> rimraf ../dist/


> build
> build-storybook -c . -o ../dist --quiet --loglevel warn
